## Safety checklist

Before applying a recommendation:
1. Confirm the page intent matches the query.
2. Keep titles/descriptions truthful and specific to the page.
3. Keep one primary H1 and avoid keyword stuffing.
4. Recheck canonical/noindex settings after deployment.
